# CloudForge v4 audit — all services, every minute detail
## AWS Solutions Architect Professional (SAP-C02) + SAA-C03 lite set

Date: 2026-11-22. Baseline: docs/reference/aws-console-survey-2026-11-22.md (46/46 services, read-only; no create/edit wizard opened, no detail tabs observed for any service), docs/reference/aws-policy-editors-survey-2026-11-22.md, docs/reference/tab-audit-full-2026-11-22.md (v3.2 audit, used only as a checklist — every claim re-checked against the actual current code in ~/workspace/cloudforge).

Method: four parallel auditors, one per service group, reading the actual console code (frontend/src/pages/*.tsx, App.tsx routing, AwsTable.tsx, ServiceHome.tsx, registry backend/internal/httpapi/services.go). No code was changed by the audit. No live browser click-through (code-read only).

Verdict key: ✅ matching — present and matches the survey · ⚠️ partial — present but incomplete/different/disabled · ❌ missing — survey records it, code has no trace · ➕ invented — CloudForge-only, no survey counterpart · — survey-does-not-record (SDR) — the survey records nothing to compare against, so it cannot be verified either way. All detail tabs and all wizard steps are SDR for every service, because the survey observed none of them.

## Overall counts (per group, as counted by each auditor — counting bases differ slightly per group, see each part)

| Group | Match | Partial | Missing | Invented | SDR |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A — core (EC2, S3, VPC, IAM, Lambda, RDS, ELB, Route 53, DynamoDB, ElastiCache) | 80 | 56 | 54 | 11 | 121 |
| B — messaging/serverless/containers/devtools (SQS, SNS, EventBridge, API Gateway, ECS, Fargate, ECR, CodeBuild, CodePipeline, CodeDeploy, CloudFormation, EKS) | 34 | 24 | 74 | 8 | 24 |
| C — security/monitoring/management (KMS, Secrets, WAF, GuardDuty, Security Hub, Inspector, Config, Organizations, Cost Explorer, Budgets, Trusted Advisor, SSM, CloudTrail, CloudWatch) | 15 | 19 | 71 | 15 | 142 |
| D — remaining lite + VPC sections | — | — | — | — | — |
| **Total (A+B+C)** | **129** | **99** | **199** | **34** | **287** |

No service fully matches AWS. Group D: no survey-recorded list column missing anywhere (84 columns: 74 matching, 10 partial, 0 missing); 17 of 31 services unverifiable by design.

## Cross-cutting findings (all groups)

1. **No pagination on any list** — AwsTable has no page control (its docstring claims otherwise). Preferences gear disabled everywhere. No search on many lists (Lambda, ELB, RDS, Route 53, DynamoDB, ElastiCache, S3 search-by-name absent).
2. **No detail tabs, wizards, search, pagination anywhere in Group C; no toolbar anywhere in Group B; no wizard anywhere in Group B** — every create is a single inline form in Group B; Group A detail is stacked cards in several consoles (RDS, DynamoDB).
3. **Template-string bug is widespread, not just DataSync.** The DataSync lines the task named (MigrationConsoles.tsx:106/:358) are already correct unquoted ternaries in the current tree — the host's live literal came from an older deployed build. The same bug exists in 20 places in frontend/src: a double-quoted string containing a ternary, rendered verbatim by ServiceHomeDashboard. Group D: NetworkingConsoles.tsx:281, :420, :586. Group C: SecurityConsoles.tsx:296,431,583,737,873, ManagementConsoles.tsx:166,479, plus RdsConsole.tsx:115, ElastiCacheConsole.tsx:119, Route53Console.tsx:100, SqsConsole.tsx:109, SnsConsole.tsx:111, EventBridgeConsole.tsx:86, ApiGatewayConsole.tsx:125, EcsConsole.tsx:202, AnalyticsConsoles.tsx:337,478. Fix is dropping the surrounding double quotes.
4. **Stale version string.** Source: backend/internal/httpapi/httpapi.go:46 const Version = "0.9.0-m9", served by GET /api/v1/health and displayed on the main Dashboard (Dashboard.tsx:162). Also in console-home.test.tsx:46,70, Labs.test.tsx:28, docs/api/reference.md:10. Do not touch frontend/package.json 0.1.0 or host-agent 0.3.0-m3 (separate versions).
5. **IAM policy list exists in code but is admin-gated.** A real policy list table exists (IamConsole.tsx:1012–1014). The live host showed no list because a non-admin sees the red "IAM management is admin-only" banner instead — needs a live re-check as admin, not just a code fix. Policy detail still has none of AWS's 4 tabs (Permissions Summary/JSON, Entities attached, Policy versions, Last Accessed) — detail is a JSON dump + Delete. IAM editor still lacks 6 survey features: access-level action groups with counts, MFA/IP conditions, Policy Linter, Import policy, Review step with tags, Actions dropdown.
6. **VPC's 4 missing tabs persist, verified by grep:** Verified Access, Cloud WAN, DNS Firewall, Lattice (only the "PrivateLink and Lattice" group label exists, no Lattice item).
7. **Registry contradicts the consoles** (Group D §35): services.go advertises API_EMULATED backends with create wizards/actions for VPN ("Tunnel activate/deactivate"), EBS ("Create-volume wizard; Attach/Detach"), Auto Scaling ("Create-group wizard; Scale action") — none exist; consoles honestly say UNSUPPORTED.
8. **v3.2 fixes verified still present:** all 27 EC2 nav labels, zero label-only tabs; S3 second half; /services/nacl → VPC NACLs redirect; /services/budgets → BudgetsConsole; all 17 exam services have dedicated consoles (not generic); no survey-recorded list column missing in Group D.
9. **v3.2 audit corrections found by this audit:** several v3.2 "sections" were Home cards only, not sections (SQS/SNS/EventBridge/API Gateway/ECS; GuardDuty Suppression rules, Secrets Versions/Rotation, KMS Aliases/Grants, WAF Rules/Rule groups — only Config's three are real tables). CodeDeploy backup note is wrong twice (claims deployment groups exist; claims EC2 labelled unavailable — neither true). CloudWatch backup note is false (claims Logs/Dashboards nav, metric graphs, Create-alarm wizard — none exist in code). ECR backup note overstates lifecycle rules (no rules table, only create-form fields + Evaluate).
10. **Docstring drift** in RDS ("detail tabs"), ELB ("Listeners and Target Groups tabs"), Route 53 ("Create-record wizard"), DynamoDB ("Overview/Items tabs, Create-table wizard") — headers describe UI the code does not render.

## Part A — Group A — core compute/storage/networking (EC2, S3, VPC, IAM, Lambda, RDS, ELB, Route 53, DynamoDB, ElastiCache)

Counts: 80 match, 56 partial, 54 missing, 11 invented, 121 survey-does-not-record

# CloudForge v4 audit — Group A: core compute / storage / networking

Date: 2026-11-22. Auditor: subagent (Group A).
Scope: EC2, S3, VPC, IAM, Lambda, RDS, ELB, Route 53, DynamoDB, ElastiCache.
Method: read the actual console code in `frontend/src/pages/*.tsx` (and
`frontend/src/App.tsx` routing, `frontend/src/components/AwsTable.tsx`,
`frontend/src/services/iamPolicyEditor.ts`, `frontend/src/pages/NetworkingConsoles.tsx`
for the TGW section). Every claim below was re-checked against current code;
the v3.2 audit (`docs/reference/tab-audit-full-2026-11-22.md`) was used only as
a checklist of claims to verify, not as evidence.

Baselines:
- `docs/reference/aws-console-survey-2026-11-22.md` (46/46 services, ap-southeast-2,
  read-only; **no create/edit wizard was opened, no detail tabs were observed for
  any service** — tables were empty or the list page was never reached with rows)
- `docs/reference/aws-policy-editors-survey-2026-11-22.md` (IAM Create policy is the
  only Visual+JSON editor; policy detail tabs recorded for one managed policy)

Verdict key (per audited item):
- ✅ **matching** — present and matches the survey
- ⚠️ **partial** — present but incomplete, different content, or disabled/label-only
  (honest empty state still counts as partial, not matching, unless the survey
  itself shows an empty table)
- ❌ **missing** — survey records it, code has no trace of it
- ➕ **invented** — CloudForge-only item with no survey counterpart
- — **survey-does-not-record (SDR)** — the survey records nothing to compare against,
  so the item cannot be verified either way. This is the verdict for **all detail
  tabs and all wizard steps**: the survey observed none of them.

## Cross-cutting findings (apply to the whole group)

1. **No pagination anywhere.** The survey records a pagination control on the
   S3, IAM, Lambda, ELB, Budgets and other lists. `AwsTable.tsx` renders only a
   "{rows} of {total} shown" footer (L62) and a "(filtered from N)" note (L128);
   there is no page control in any Group A console. Every list that the survey
   shows with pagination is therefore partial on controls.
2. **Preferences gear is present but disabled everywhere** (`AwsPreferencesButton
   disabledReason="Table preferences are not persisted in CloudForge."`). The
   survey records a Preferences gear on S3/Lambda/ELB lists — present as a
   disabled control, i.e. partial.
3. **"Actions" extra column.** S3 Buckets, Lambda Functions, RDS Databases,
   Route 53 Zones/Records, DynamoDB Tables/Items, ElastiCache Clusters, ELB
   Load Balancers and TGW Attachments all add an `Actions` column the survey
   does not record. It is a consistent CloudForge pattern, not an AWS match.
4. **Docstring drift.** Three consoles' header comments claim tab sets the code
   does not render (see §6 RDS, §7 ELB, §8 Route 53, §9 DynamoDB). The code,
   not the comment, is audited here.
5. **No wizard steps are survey-verified for any service** (survey opened no
   wizard). EC2, S3 and Lambda wizards all carry the "steps inferred, not
   observed" footnote in code — correctly. RDS, Route 53, DynamoDB and
   ElastiCache use inline create forms, not wizards.

## Summary counts

Counting basis: one count per survey nav item, per survey-recorded list column,
per survey-recorded detail tab, per survey-recorded control/entry point, plus
per invented item found in code. Detail tabs and wizard steps are SDR for every
service (survey observed none) and are counted in the SDR column, not as
matches. "Partial" nav items include honest UNSUPPORTED empty sections and
disabled labels — the label exists, AWS-equivalent content does not.

| Service | ✅ Matching | ⚠️ Partial | ❌ Missing | ➕ Invented | — SDR |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EC2 | 9 | 19 | 0 | 0 | 23 |
| S3 | 9 | 2 | 0 | 2 | 19 |
| VPC | 31 | 15 | 3 | 2 | ~20 |
| IAM | 8 | 3 | 10 | 2 | 14 |
| Lambda | 7 | 4 | 4 | 2 | 10 |
| RDS | 2 | 2 | 15 | 1 | 6 |
| ELB | 12 | 1 | 5 | 1 | 4 |
| Route 53 | 1 | 5 | 9 | 1 | 8 |
| DynamoDB | 0 | 1 | 1 | 0 | 7 |
| ElastiCache | 1 | 4 | 7 | 0 | 10 |
| **Group A total** | **80** | **56** | **54** | **11** | **121** |

Headline: the v3.2 fixes verified as still present — all 27 EC2 nav labels,
S3 second-half sections, NACLs section + `/services/nacl` redirect,
`/services/budgets` → `BudgetsConsole`, IAM policy list. The v3.2 gaps that
remain: VPC's 4 missing tabs, IAM policy detail tabs, RDS's 13 missing nav
items, Route 53's missing nav. New findings in this audit: the live-host
"no IAM policy list" report is **contradicted by the code** (a real list
exists; see §4); DynamoDB has **no home page and no left nav at all**; three
consoles' docstrings claim tabs the code does not render.

## 1. EC2 — `frontend/src/pages/Ec2Console.tsx` (1141 lines)

Fidelity label: PARTIAL ("labs SIMULATED · no real AWS hardware").
Route: `App.tsx:77` — `<Ec2Console initialSection="home" />`, so it lands on
the EC2 dashboard, matching the survey (Home is an overview page, no table).

### 1a. Left nav — all 27 survey items present as labels (subNav L204–227)

| # | AWS nav item (survey) | CloudForge | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | EC2 dashboard | `home` — ServiceHomeDashboard, 8 cards | ✅ |
| 2 | Instances | Real lab list | ✅ |
| 3 | Instance Types | Lab profiles table; vCPU/memory "Not modelled" | ✅ |
| 4 | Launch Templates | Honest UNSUPPORTED section, disabled Create | ⚠️ |
| 5 | Spot Requests | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 6 | Savings Plans | Link to `/services/savingsplans`, not a section | ⚠️ |
| 7 | Reserved Instances | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 8 | Dedicated Hosts | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 9 | Capacity Reservations | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 10 | AMIs | Lab definitions as images | ✅ |
| 11 | AMI Catalog | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 12 | EBS Snapshots (Images group) | Honest UNSUPPORTED section (shared with #15) | ⚠️ |
| 13 | Public Images | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 14 | Volumes (EBS) | Empty table, survey's exact 23 columns, UNSUPPORTED | ✅ |
| 15 | Snapshots (EBS group) | Honest UNSUPPORTED section (shared with #12) | ⚠️ |
| 16 | Lifecycle Manager | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 17 | Security Groups | Lists only SGs from loaded instances; management deferred to VPC | ✅ |
| 18 | Elastic IPs | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 19 | Placement Groups | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 20 | Key Pairs | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 21 | Network Interfaces | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 22 | Load Balancers | `ElbListContent`, SIMULATED | ✅ |
| 23 | Target Groups | Same `ElbListContent` as Load Balancers | ✅ |
| 24 | Trust Stores | Honest UNSUPPORTED section | ⚠️ |
| 25 | Auto Scaling Groups | UNSUPPORTED empty state, Create disabled | ✅ |

| 27 | Account settings | Link to `/account`, not a section | ⚠️ |

**v3.2 claim "zero label-only tabs" VERIFIED**: every label renders a section
or a link; the old 5 label-only items (Launch Templates, Snapshots, Lifecycle
Manager, Elastic IPs, Trust Stores) are now real UNSUPPORTED sections via
`EmptySection` (L~960). But "present" ≠ "matching": only 9 of 27 render
AWS-equivalent content; 15 are honest empty states and 3 are outbound links.
Nav counts: ✅ 9, ⚠️ 18, ❌ 0, ➕ 0.
Note: the two Snapshots nav items render the *same* section (code comment
says the survey lists the list twice) — defensible, and the survey does record
both items.

### 1b. Home dashboard (L~240)

Survey: hero with Launch instance button + 5 cards (Virtual machines, High
performance computing, Machine learning and AI, Serverless compute, Storage
and data). CloudForge: ServiceHomeDashboard with Launch instance action +
8 cards (Instances, AMIs, Volumes, Load Balancers, Auto Scaling Groups,
Elastic IPs, Security Groups, Launch Templates) with real counts where known,
plus health / settings / info rows. Same pattern, CloudForge's own cards.
⚠️ partial (1 item).

### 1c. Instances list — columns (L504–525), in order

Instance ID (link) | Name | Instance state | Instance type (= lab
definition id) | VPC | Subnet | Availability Zone (hard-coded
`cf-local-1a`) | Launch time | VMs (instance-resource count).
**Survey does not record EC2 Instances columns** (Home only) → all 9 — SDR.
Toolbar: Instance state menu (Start/Stop/Reboot/Terminate — real via lab API,
selection- and state-gated with reasons), Actions menu (Connect disabled "No
EC2 Instance Connect / SSH bridge"; Monitor and troubleshoot / Manage tags
switch detail tabs; Create image disabled; Attach IAM role disabled),
Refresh, Preferences disabled. Text filter (ID/name/type/VPC) + Instance-state
filter (all/ready/running/stopped/failed — **no destroying/destroyed option**,
so DESTROYING/DESTROYED labs cannot be filtered to). No search box labelled
as AWS's, no pagination (cross-cutting). Stats row: Instances / Running-ready /
Stopped / Region. Empty state: "No instances match this filter — launch one,
or clear the filter." Load error: `ErrorBox` with the real error message.

### 1d. Detail tabs on a selected instance (L533–545), in order

Details | Security | Networking | Storage | Monitoring | Status checks |
Logs | Tags | Events — **survey does not record any EC2 detail tabs → all 9 SDR**.
Content per tab: Details — real lab fields (ID, name, state, type, region/AZ,
launch time, lab resources, provisioning error). Security — SG resources of
the lab only. Networking — VPC/subnet/gateway/route-table resources only.
Storage — UNSUPPORTED note only, no volumes. Monitoring — control-plane
metrics from `/api/v1/monitoring`, explicitly "No per-instance CloudWatch-style
metrics pipeline exists". Status checks — derived from lab state + engine
reachability. Logs and Events — **both render the same `InstanceEvents`
component (identical audit-event table)** — duplicated content. Tags — real tag
editing via `setResourceTags` (resource picker, add/remove, `aws:` keys
rejected). No detail-tab set can be called an AWS match.

### 1e. Launch instance wizard (L88, L289–~420)

5 steps: Name and tags | Application and OS Images | Instance type |
Network settings | Summary. Fields: name (optional); image = lab-definition
radio list (lite blocks >30 min definitions with "Not available in lite
mode"); instance type = profile id only, no specs ("vCPU/memory specs are
not modelled"); network settings = informational only, no choices; summary
rows (name, image, type, network, region). No per-step validation beyond
empty-name defaulting; footnote "wizard steps inferred … not observed"
present. **All 5 steps — SDR** (survey opened no wizard).
Other sections: AMIs table (Image ID / Name / Duration / Lite mode /
Description, L613–617); Instance types table (Profile / Name / Max duration /
Specs="Not modelled", L639–642); Volumes table 23/23 survey columns, all
empty (L719+); Security Groups = plain list, not a table, no create/edit.

## 2. S3 — `frontend/src/pages/S3Console.tsx` (903 lines)

Fidelity label: PARTIAL ("buckets + objects over HTTP · MinIO-backed").
Route: `App.tsx:78` — `<S3Console initialSection="buckets" />`, lands on the
Buckets list, matching the survey.

### 2a. Left nav (subNav L117–128)

| AWS nav item (survey) | CloudForge | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Buckets (General purpose / Directory / Table / Vector buckets) | One list, **no bucket-type sub-tabs** | ⚠️ |
| Files (File systems) | Real UNSUPPORTED section + "What you can do instead" | ✅ |
| Access Points | Disabled label only, "Not supported by the local adapter", **no section renders** | ⚠️ |
| Access Grants | Real UNSUPPORTED section | ✅ |
| IAM Access Analyzer | Real UNSUPPORTED section (Create analyzer disabled) | ✅ |
| Storage management and insights | Real PARTIAL table (see §2d) | ✅ |
| Storage Lens | Real UNSUPPORTED section (Create dashboard disabled) | ✅ |

Nav counts: ✅ 5, ⚠️ 2, ❌ 0. **v3.2 claim "S3 second half added" VERIFIED** —
Files / Access Grants / Analyzer / Storage Lens / Storage management all
render sections now (previously missing). Remaining gap is only the bucket-type
sub-tabs and the label-only Access Points.
➕ **Invented: `S3 dashboard` home tab** — the survey records no S3 dashboard
(S3 lands on the Buckets list); CloudForge adds a Home dashboard (4 cards:
Buckets, Objects, Access Points, Block Public Access). The other extra tabs
(Object Lambda access points, Batch operations, Replication — disabled labels;
Block Public Access settings — informational card) are **not recorded in the
survey as nav items either way → SDR (4 items)**, not counted as invented,
since no AWS detail may be invented about them.

### 2b. Buckets list — columns (L335–353), in order

Name (link) | Region (hard-coded `cf-local-1`) | IAM Access Analyzer
(always "Not available") | Creation date | **Actions (Open / Delete)**.
Survey columns 4/4 ✅ matching; Actions column ➕ invented (1).
Toolbar: Actions menu (Delete gated on selection; Empty bucket disabled "No
bulk-empty operation"; Copy bucket name; Create access point disabled),
Refresh, Preferences disabled. **No search-by-name box** (survey records one)
and no pagination — controls partial. Create bucket button is top-level and
disabled with reason when MinIO is down; MinIO down renders an honest
`ErrorBox` ("no buckets are shown as a fake empty list") instead of an empty
table. Stats row: Buckets / Selected / Backend (MinIO) / Region. Delete works
only on empty buckets (real MinIO constraint, stated under the table).
Empty state: "No buckets yet — choose Create bucket to make one."

### 2c. Bucket detail tabs (L383–~395), in order

Objects | Properties | Permissions | Management | Metrics — **survey does
not record any S3 bucket detail tabs → all 5 SDR**.
- Objects: real list/upload/download/delete via backend (10 MiB cap, prefix
  breadcrumbs, folder navigation); columns Key | Size | Last modified | Type |
  Actions (L671–683); object detail panel shows Key/Size/Content type/ETag/
  Last modified/User metadata. Actions menu Download / Copy S3 URI / Delete /
  Open, all selection-gated.
- Properties: real metadata + real versioning toggle (MinIO-backed).
- Permissions: JSON bucket-policy editor (fetch/save via backend); object ACLs
  explicitly "not available".
- Management: versioning repeat + "No replication or lifecycle rules" note.
- Metrics: placeholder paragraph linking to CloudWatch.

### 2d. Storage management and insights section (L~850–903)

Real PARTIAL table, columns Bucket | Versioning (real per-bucket backend read)
| Replication rules ("None — no replication API") | Lifecycle rules ("None —
no lifecycle API") | Inventory ("Not available"). Columns are CloudForge's own
(code says so) — **SDR (5 columns)**, not claimed as an AWS match.

### 2e. Create bucket wizard (L64, L204–~300)

5 steps: General configuration | Ownership | Block Public Access |
Versioning | Summary. Fields: bucket name (regex-validated, inline error "That
name does not meet the local naming rules"); Region = disabled select
(`cf-local-1` only). Ownership, BPA and Versioning steps are **informational
only** (no ACL/BPA surface; versioning "is not set during this wizard").
Summary rows: name / region / backend / versioning off. Footnote "steps
inferred, not observed" present. **All 5 steps — SDR.**

S3 counts: ✅ 9 (5 nav + 4 columns), ⚠️ 2, ❌ 0, ➕ 2 (dashboard tab, Actions
column), — SDR 19 (5 detail tabs + 5 objects columns + 5 wizard steps + 4
unrecorded tabs).

### 3b. Home dashboard (L~230)

ServiceHomeDashboard with 11 cards (Your VPCs, Subnets, Route Tables,
Internet Gateways, NAT Gateways, Security Groups, Network ACLs, Elastic IPs,

### 3c. List columns

- Generic sections (VPCs/Subnets/Route Tables/Gateways/NAT/SGs, L367–371):
  ID (link) | Name | CIDR | State | Parent | Fidelity. **Survey records no
  VPC list columns → all 6 SDR.** Toolbar: Actions menu (Delete selected,
  View lab topology), Refresh, Preferences disabled. No search/filter box.
  Create form per section: Name + CIDR (VPCs/Subnets) + parent-VPC select
  (Subnets/Route Tables) + gateway-type select (IGWs); backend validates CIDR
  (400 on bad CIDR, stated). No create/edit wizard — inline form.

### 4b. IAM policy list + policy detail — the live-host finding, checked in code

**The 2026-11-22 21:40 IST live-host check reported "no policy list anywhere".
That is CONTRADICTED by the code.** `PoliciesSection` (L~800–1090) renders a
real policies table (L1012–1014): Policy name (link) | Description | Created,
with rows from `fetchIamPolicies`, an empty state ("No IAM policies yet —
create one above"), and clicking a name opens a detail card. Likely
explanations for the live observation, in code: (a) IAM is **admin-only** —
a non-admin sees the red banner "IAM management is admin-only (admin role
required)" and `AdminOnly` replaces every list with that error (L~150–155);
the host check did report exactly that banner and a disabled Create policy
button; (b) the check may have stayed on the Dashboard section, where only
counts and links are shown. Either way, **the list exists in code**; the host
finding needs re-checking as an admin, not a code fix.

**Policy detail, however, is still missing the AWS tabs.** Clicking a policy
renders only a `<pre>` JSON dump of the document + a Delete button (L~1070).
The policy-editor survey records these detail tabs for an existing policy
(AdministratorAccess): Permissions (Summary/JSON segmented control),
Entities attached, Policy versions, Last Accessed. In code: Permissions —
⚠️ partial (raw JSON view present, no Summary view, no tab structure);
Entities attached — ❌ missing (no attach list on the policy; attach/detach
happens only from the user/group/role detail); Policy versions — ❌ missing
(no version concept at all); Last Accessed — ❌ missing (no activity data).

| VPC: missing Verified Access, Cloud WAN, PrivateLink and Lattice, DNS Firewall | **Still true, verified by grep** — none of the four appears in `VpcConsole.tsx` except the "PrivateLink and Lattice" *group label* (L159–160) with no Lattice item. |
| IAM: policy detail is JSON-only, 4 AWS tabs missing | **Still true** — detail is a JSON `<pre>` + Delete (L~1070); no Permissions/Entities/Versions/Last Accessed tabs (grep: no match). |
| IAM: no policy list (live-host check 21:40 IST) | **Contradicted by code** — a real policy list table exists (L1012–1014). See §4b for the likely admin-only explanation. Needs a live re-check as admin. |
| Budgets/NACLs land on the wrong page | **Fixed, verified in routing** — `App.tsx:80` `/services/nacl` → `/services/vpc?section=nacls`; `App.tsx:130` `/services/budgets` → `BudgetsConsole`. (Budgets itself is Group C scope; only the routing was checked here.) |
| DataSync columns mismatch | Out of Group A scope; not re-checked. |
| "No service fully matches AWS" | **Still true for Group A** — best are S3 (list columns 4/4) and ELB (11/11), but every service is missing nav items, detail tabs, or both; and detail tabs are unverifiable for all ten because the survey observed none. |
| Detail tabs / wizard steps unvalidated | **Still true** — the survey records no detail tabs and no wizard steps for any Group A service; all CloudForge detail tabs (EC2 9, S3 5, Lambda 6) and wizards (EC2 5 steps, S3 5, Lambda 4) are CloudForge's own design. |

## 12. Top gaps (Group A, ranked)

1. **RDS is a two-view console, not the 18-item AWS nav.** 13 of 18 survey nav
   items have no trace (Exports, Automated backups, Reserved instances,
   Capacity reservations, Proxies, Subnet groups, Option groups, Custom engine
   versions, Cluster endpoints, Events, Event subscriptions, Integrations,
   Recommendations). Only Dashboard + Databases exist, plus a detail-only
   Snapshots card and a disabled Parameter-groups card.
2. **Route 53 has no left nav at all** — 9 of 14 survey items missing
   (Domains, Traffic flow/policies/records, DNS Firewall, Resolver
   Rules/Endpoints/Profiles as separate items, Home/Dashboard entries).
   Same structural gap in ElastiCache (7 of 11 missing) and DynamoDB (no
   home, no nav).
3. **IAM policy detail has none of AWS's 4 tabs** (Permissions Summary/JSON,
   Entities attached, Policy versions, Last Accessed) — detail is a JSON dump
   + Delete. The policy *list* does exist in code, contradicting the live-host
   report; the admin-only gate is the likely cause of that observation.
4. **IAM policy editor still lacks 6 of the survey's editor features**:
   access-level action groups with counts, MFA/IP conditions, Policy Linter,
   Import policy, Review step with tags, Actions dropdown. (Core Visual+JSON
   tabs match.)
5. **VPC's 4 missing tabs persist**: Verified Access, Cloud WAN, DNS Firewall,
   and Lattice (group label only). VPC is otherwise the strongest nav in the
   group (8/25 fully matching, 19/19 VPN columns, 4/4 TGW attachment columns).
6. **Lambda is missing Applications and Replicas**, labels its list
   "Functions" instead of the survey's "Event Functions", has no list search,
   and its Code tab is read-only (no editor) while the survey's landing hero
   (language tabs + code-editor panel) is not reproduced.
7. **EC2's 18 non-content nav items are honest but empty.** All 27 labels
   exist, but 15 are UNSUPPORTED sections and 3 are outbound links; only 9
   render content. Logs and Events detail tabs duplicate the same table.
8. **No pagination on any list; Preferences gear disabled everywhere; no
   search on Lambda/ELB/RDS/Route 53/DynamoDB/ElastiCache lists** (S3's
   survey-recorded search-by-name is also absent). These are small individually
   but affect every service's toolbar fidelity.
9. **Docstring drift in 4 files** (RDS "detail tabs", ELB "Listeners and
   Target Groups tabs", Route 53 "Create-record wizard", DynamoDB "Overview /
   Items tabs, Create-table wizard") — the headers describe UI the code does
   not render. Harmless to users, misleading to the next auditor.
10. **Detail tabs remain unverifiable everywhere.** The survey observed detail
    tabs for no service, so EC2's 9, S3's 5 and Lambda's 6 detail tabs — and
    the stacked-card details in RDS/Route 53/DynamoDB/ElastiCache — cannot be
    called matches. Any v4 claim of "fully matching AWS" for a service would
    need a fresh survey that actually opens rows.

## 13. What was NOT checked (out of scope / needs a live host)

- Whether the IAM policy list renders on the real Fedora host as admin
  (code says yes; live check said no list — needs an admin-session re-check).
- TGW Route tables / Policy tables and the full VPN tab set beyond the
  connections table (only the attachments columns were re-verified here).
- `survey-reconciliation.test.tsx` assertions were read, not run (no builds
  per task); the test file's Group A assertions (S3, IAM Roles, Lambda,
  ELB, TGW attachments, EBS volumes, EC2 nav, VPC VPN/NACLs) are consistent
  with the code read here.
- No live-browser AWS re-check was done; nothing here invents AWS details
  beyond the two survey files.


## Part B — Group B — messaging/serverless/containers/devtools (SQS, SNS, EventBridge, API Gateway, ECS, Fargate, ECR, CodeBuild, CodePipeline, CodeDeploy, CloudFormation, EKS)

Counts: 34 match, 24 partial, 74 missing, 8 invented, 24 survey-does-not-record (survey-based totals; ECR/CodeDeploy/Fargate excluded, backup-note-based)

# CloudForge v4 audit — Group B: messaging, serverless, containers, developer tools

Date: 2026-11-22. Auditor: subagent (Group B). Scope: SQS, SNS, EventBridge,
API Gateway, ECS, Fargate, ECR, CodeBuild, CodePipeline, CodeDeploy,
CloudFormation, EKS.

Sources (read first, as instructed):
- `docs/reference/aws-console-survey-2026-11-22.md` (46/46 services,
  ap-southeast-2, read-only; **no create/edit wizard was opened**, so no wizard
  steps are recorded for any service; detail tabs were almost never observed —
  tables were empty or the list page was never reached with rows).
- `docs/reference/tab-audit-full-2026-11-22.md` (v3.2 audit) — **every claim
  re-checked against the actual current code below; corrections are flagged
  `V3.2-AUDIT CORRECTION`**.
- `docs/reference/aws-console-backup/*.md` — used ONLY where the survey records
  nothing (ECR, CodeDeploy, and detail-tab/wizard shape for CodeBuild,
  CodePipeline, CloudFormation, EKS). Backup notes are explicitly "public
  console structure" reference, **not** observed-survey evidence; every verdict
  resting on them is marked `(backup, not survey)`.
- Code read in full: `frontend/src/pages/SqsConsole.tsx` (263 lines),
  `SnsConsole.tsx` (258), `EventBridgeConsole.tsx` (206),
  `ApiGatewayConsole.tsx` (310), `EcsConsole.tsx` (471), `EcrConsole.tsx`
  (241), `CodeBuildConsole.tsx` (206), `CodePipelineConsole.tsx` (227),
  `CodeDeployConsole.tsx` (201), `CloudFormationConsole.tsx` (235),
  `EksConsole.tsx` (106); `frontend/src/App.tsx` routes (lines 80–154);
  `frontend/src/components/ServiceHome.tsx` (`ConsoleWithHome`,
  `ServiceHomeDashboard`); `frontend/src/components/AwsTable.tsx`;
  registry `backend/internal/httpapi/services.go` (fidelity values).

## Verdict key

- ✅ **match** — present and agreeing with the survey as recorded.
- ⚠️ **partial** — present but incomplete, disabled-by-design, honest
  "Not available", or present in the wrong place (e.g. a detail card instead
  of a nav item).
- ❌ **missing** — recorded in the survey (or backup note) and absent.
- ➕ **invented** — CloudForge-only element with no survey counterpart.
- — **survey-does-not-record (SDR)** — the survey records nothing to compare
  against; no verdict is possible. CloudForge's content here is its own
  design, **not** claimed as a match. This applies to list columns for most
  services, detail tabs for ALL services, and wizard steps for ALL services.

## Global findings (apply to all 12 services)

1. **No left nav anywhere in Group B.** The five `ConsoleWithHome` consoles
   (SQS, SNS, EventBridge, API Gateway, ECS) render exactly two tabs —
   `home` and `list` (`ServiceHome.tsx:258-259`: `{ id: 'home', ... },
   { id: 'list', ... }`). There is no per-service left navigation panel at
   all, so every AWS nav item beyond the list page is either a Home-dashboard
   card, a detail card under a selected row, or absent. The other seven
   consoles (ECR, CodeBuild, CodePipeline, CodeDeploy, CloudFormation, EKS)
   use bare `PageHeader` with **no tabs at all** — a single scrolling page.
2. **No search / filter / checkbox / pagination / Preferences gear anywhere
   in Group B.** `AwsTable` supports all of them (`filter` + `onFilterChange`
   renders a search input, `selectable` renders a checkbox column), but a
   grep over all 11 console files finds **zero** uses of `onFilterChange`,
   `selectable`, `selectedIds`, or `filter` props. Every table is an
   unfiltered full list with only a "`N items`" footer. This alone makes
   every survey toolbar (search, Filter status/type, Refresh, Actions
   dropdown, Preferences gear, pagination) missing in every service.
3. **No create/edit wizard anywhere in Group B.** Every create flow is a
   single inline `<form>` on the list page (name + a few fields). The survey
   opened no wizard, so wizard fidelity is SDR for all services — but
   (backup, not survey) the AWS wizards are multi-step (CodeBuild 7 steps,
   CodePipeline 5, CloudFormation 4, EKS 5, ECR 5), and CloudForge implements
   none of those steps.
4. **No detail tabs anywhere in Group B.** Detail is rendered as stacked
   `AwsCard` sections under a selected row (SQS queue detail, SNS
   subscriptions/publish, API Gateway resources/invoke/logs, ECS has no
   row-detail at all, ECR image detail block, CodeDeploy deployment detail
   block, CloudFormation resources/events/change-set sections). The survey
   records detail tabs for **no** service, so all of these are SDR — but
   (backup, not survey) AWS detail-tab sets exist for ECR, CodeBuild,
   CodePipeline, CloudFormation and EKS, and none of them is implemented.
5. **Error / empty states are uniform and honest.** Load failure →
   `ErrorBox` ("X could not be loaded (…message…)"); empty table →
   `AwsTable` `emptyMessage` (e.g. SQS "No queues yet. Create one above.",
   EKS "No clusters — EKS is UNSUPPORTED in CloudForge."); action
   success/failure → a `<p role="status">` notice line. No fake data: counts
   are real query lengths, "—" is shown where there is no count API.
6. **Fidelity labels (registry `services.go` + console badge):** SQS PARTIAL,
   SNS PARTIAL, EventBridge PARTIAL, API Gateway PARTIAL, ECS PARTIAL,
   Fargate SIMULATED, ECR PARTIAL, CodeBuild PARTIAL, CodePipeline PARTIAL,
   CodeDeploy PARTIAL, CloudFormation PARTIAL, EKS UNSUPPORTED. All 12
   console badges match their registry values (checked: each file's
   `fidelity=` / `FidelityBadge` prop).
7. **v3.2 re-check.** The v3.2 fix list (`tab-audit-full §6`) covers only
   Budgets/NACL routing, DataSync columns, the 17 generic consoles, EC2
   nav, and S3's second half. **No Group B service is in the v3.2 fix list**,
   and the routes confirm v3.2 is applied (`App.tsx`: `/services/budgets` →
   `BudgetsConsole`, `/services/nacl` → redirect to VPC). Group B code is
   therefore as the v3.2 audit saw it, except for the corrections flagged
   below — most of which are cases where the v3.2 audit overstated a home
   card or detail card as a nav section/tab.

---

## 1. SQS — `frontend/src/pages/SqsConsole.tsx`, route `/services/sqs`

Registry fidelity PARTIAL (`services.go:657`). Console badge PARTIAL,
footnote "durable Postgres queues · no FIFO".

### Survey (recorded)
Home is a marketing page. Left nav is a **single item: Queues**. No table on
home. No list columns, no detail tabs, no wizard recorded.

### Left nav
| AWS nav item (survey) | CloudForge | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Queues | `listLabel="Queues"` — the list tab (`SqsConsole.tsx:110`) | ✅ match |

### Home dashboard (`ServiceHomeDashboard`, lines 111–139)
Banner: "Durable Postgres queues: visibility timeout, retention, DLQ redrive
after max receives are real. No FIFO queues, no long polling." Actions:
"Create queue" (primary, jumps to list), "View SNS topics (publisher)".
Cards (Resources by Region): Queues (real count), Messages (selected queue)
(count or "—"), Dead-letter queues (count "—", note "DLQ view on the queue
detail"), FIFO queues (count 0, `disabledReason: 'No FIFO queues in
CloudForge.'`). Health lines: queue count or load error; "Durable Postgres
queues — the old in-memory simulator is replaced." Settings: Resource
Explorer, All services. Info: Learning Center — messaging track, EventBridge.
- Home page itself: survey says marketing page; CloudForge shows the
  VPC-pattern dashboard instead → ⚠️ partial.
- FIFO card: AWS has FIFO queues; CloudForge honestly labels them absent →
  ⚠️ partial (honest missing, not a match).

### List page — Queues table (lines 145–225)
Single inline create form, **not a wizard**: Queue name (text, placeholder
"orders"), Visibility timeout (s) (number, min 1 max 43200, default 30), Max
receives (0 = no DLQ) (number, min 0, default 0), DLQ queue id (text,
optional). Submit "Create queue" (disabled while pending; no-op on empty
name). Validation is client-side only (empty-name guard); no error text for
bad timeout/DLQ id beyond the API error notice.
Columns, in order: Queue name (link → selects queue), Visible messages,
Invisible messages, Visibility timeout, Max receives, Actions (Redrive DLQ /
Purge / Delete per row). No checkbox, no search, no filter, no pagination,
no Refresh/Actions toolbar, no Preferences gear.
- Columns: — SDR (survey records no SQS columns).
- Toolbar: no survey toolbar recorded → SDR for the toolbar itself, but the
  **absence of search/filter/pagination** is a global gap (§Global 2).
- Redrive DLQ button: disabled when the queue has no `dead_letter_queue_id`
  (no title/reason shown — plain `disabled`). Purge and Delete always
  enabled. All three are per-row buttons, not a toolbar.
- Empty state: "No queues yet. Create one above."

### Detail — selected queue (lines 227–263)
No tabs. One card "Queue detail — {name}": DLQ name (or "none configured"),
Retention seconds. Send-message form: Message body (text, default
`{"order": 1}`), "Send message" + "Receive (1)" + "Refresh messages"
buttons. Note: "Receive hides the message until the visibility timeout — it
does not delete it." Messages table columns: Message id, Body, Receive
count, Visible ("yes" / "no (in flight)"), Actions (Delete per message).
- Detail tabs: — SDR. Detail content itself (Send/Receive/DLQ redrive) is
  CloudForge's own layout, not survey-verified.

### Counts — SQS
| match | partial | missing | invented | SDR |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 (Queues nav) | 2 (home page, FIFO card) | 0 | 1 (Home dashboard pattern, where survey records a marketing page) | 3 (list columns, detail tabs, wizard steps) |

### V3.2-audit correction
The v3.2 audit (§3a) lists SQS as "Home + Queues (real, PARTIAL), Messages
(selected queue), Dead-letter queues, FIFO queues" — this overstates the
structure. Messages, DLQ and FIFO are **Home-dashboard cards only** (FIFO a
disabled card); there is no Messages section, no DLQ list view, and no FIFO
view. The only list/detail views are the Queues table and the single queue
detail card. The audit's "mostly matching" verdict still holds for the
one-item nav, but the tab count is 2 (home/list), not 4.

unmapped methods return 404 — never a fake success." (3) "Request logs
(latest 100)": columns Time, Stage, Method, Path, Status, Duration (ms).
- Detail tabs: — SDR. Resources/Stages/Invoke/Logs layout is CloudForge's
  own; the survey records no API Gateway detail tabs.

### V3.2-audit correction
The v3.2 audit (§3a) lists API Gateway as "Home + APIs (real, PARTIAL),
Resources, Stages, WebSocket APIs" and "partial". As with SQS/SNS/
EventBridge, Resources, Stages and WebSocket APIs are **cards, not
sections**: Resources/Stages are detail cards under a selected API,
WebSocket APIs is a disabled home card. The audit's missing list (Custom
domain names, VPC links, Usage plans, API keys, API Portal; no REST/HTTP/
WebSocket tab split) is confirmed in full.

## 5. ECS — `frontend/src/pages/EcsConsole.tsx`, route `/services/ecs`

Registry fidelity PARTIAL (`services.go:494`). Console badge PARTIAL,
footnote "subprocess runtime · no Docker · no VPC networking".

### Survey (recorded)
Get-started marketing page. Left nav: Search ECS resources, Favorites and
recents, Express Mode, Clusters, Gateways, Namespaces, Task definitions,
Daemon task definitions, Account settings. No table on get-started page. No
list columns, no detail tabs, no wizard recorded.

### Left nav
| AWS nav item (survey) | CloudForge | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| Search ECS resources | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Favorites and recents | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Express Mode | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Clusters | Clusters card on the single list page (lines 215–262) | ✅ match |
| Gateways | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Namespaces | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Task definitions | Task definitions card on the same list page (lines 264–312) | ✅ match |
| Daemon task definitions | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Account settings | Absent | ❌ missing |

There is no left nav and no section switching: the list page is four
stacked cards (Clusters, Task definitions, Services, Tasks) on one scrolling
page. Services and Tasks are not survey nav items (in AWS they live under a
cluster); the survey records nothing about them → SDR.

### Home dashboard (lines 178–209)
Banner: "Clusters, task definitions, services and tasks are real records;
the runtime is SIMULATED — a task command runs as a sandboxed subprocess on
the API host, no Docker container is started and no image is pulled."
Actions: "Create cluster" (primary, jumps to list), "Open Fargate
(serverless runs)" (→ `/services/fargate`). Cards: Clusters, Task
definitions, Services, Tasks (real counts; Tasks note "Status comes from the
real process"), Fargate (count "—", link to `/services/fargate`, note "Same
runtime, launch type FARGATE, no cluster to manage"). Health: cluster/
service/task counts or load error; "Desired count is reconciled on every
read and when a task stops; logs are real process output." Info: Learning
Center — containers track; EKS (unsupported — Kubernetes concepts).
- Home page: survey says get-started marketing page; CloudForge shows the
  dashboard → ⚠️ partial.

### List page — four cards, all inline forms (no wizards)
1. **Clusters** (lines 215–262). Create form: Cluster name + "Create
   cluster". Columns: Cluster name, Status, Running tasks, Actions (Delete;
   **disabled when running_tasks > 0**, title "Stop the running tasks (or
   delete the services) first."). Empty: "No clusters yet. Create one above."
2. **Task definitions** (lines 264–312). Register form: Family, Image
   (default "demo/app:latest"), Command (default "echo hello-task") +
   "Register task definition". Note: "CPU 256 / memory 128 MiB per container
   by default. A container definition without a command is rejected — the
   runtime needs a real command to run." Columns: Family, Revision, Image,
   Command, Actions (Deregister; **disabled when in_use_by_services > 0**,
   title "In use by an active service."). Empty: "No task definitions yet.
   Register one above."
3. **Services** (lines 314–382). Create form: Service name, Launch type
   select (EC2 / Fargate), Cluster select (**disabled when launch type is
   Fargate**), Task definition select, Desired count (number, min 0 max 10,
   default 1) + "Create service". Columns: Service name, Launch type,
   Status (plus "Throttled: …" warning line when `reconcile_throttled`),
   Desired, Running, Actions (Scale +1 / Scale −1 / Delete; **Scale +1
   disabled at desired ≥ 10, Scale −1 disabled at desired ≤ 0**). Empty:
   "No services yet. Create one above."
4. **Tasks — EC2 and Fargate runs** (lines 384–466; tasks query refetches
   every 5 s). Run-task form: Task definition select, Launch type select
   (EC2 / "Fargate (serverless — no cluster)"), Cluster select (disabled
   for Fargate), Count (1–10) + "Run task". Columns: Task, Launch type,
   Status, Exit code, Command, Actions (Logs / Stop; **Stop disabled unless
   status is RUNNING**, title "Only a RUNNING task can be stopped." — or
   "Service-managed task — scale the service instead."). Logs: clicking Logs
   renders the real process output in a `<pre>` below the table. Footer
   note: "Runtime: subprocess sandbox — timeout + output cap + temp workdir
   + prlimit memory cap + uid 65534 (nobody). Fargate runs use the same
   runtime with no cluster to manage."
- All four tables: — SDR for columns (survey records no ECS list columns).
  No checkbox, search, filter, pagination, Refresh, Preferences on any.
- No row opens a detail view; there are no detail tabs. — SDR.

### Counts — ECS
| match | partial | missing | invented | SDR |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2 (Clusters, Task definitions) | 1 (get-started page) | 7 (Search ECS resources, Favorites and recents, Express Mode, Gateways, Namespaces, Daemon task definitions, Account settings) | 0 | 5 (Services card, Tasks card, list columns, detail tabs, wizard steps) |

### V3.2-audit correction
The v3.2 audit (§3a) lists ECS as "Home + Clusters, Task definitions,
Services, Tasks, Fargate (section)". There is **no Fargate section** in the
ECS console — Fargate is a launch-type option in the Services/Tasks forms
plus a home-card link to `/services/fargate`. Services and Tasks are cards
on one page, not sections. The audit's missing list (Express Mode, Gateways,
Namespaces, Daemon task definitions, Account settings, Favorites/recents)
is confirmed, and "Search ECS resources" should be added to it.

## 6. Fargate — route `/services/fargate` → same `EcsConsole` (`App.tsx:154`)

Registry fidelity SIMULATED (`services.go:502`); registry route points at
`/services/ecs`. **The survey does not record Fargate as a console** (it is
a launch type inside ECS) — there is nothing to match against.

What exists: `App.tsx:154` renders `<EcsConsole initialSection="home" />`
at `/services/fargate` — i.e. the identical ECS console, with no
Fargate-specific view, table, or heading. Inside ECS, Fargate appears only
as (a) a home card linking to `/services/fargate`, (b) a "Fargate" option in
the Services launch-type select (which disables the Cluster select) and in
the Run-task launch-type select, and (c) the Tasks card title "EC2 and
Fargate runs". No cluster is required for Fargate runs; the runtime is the
same sandboxed subprocess.
- ➕ invented (2): the `/services/fargate` route itself (AWS has no Fargate
  console to land on) and the Fargate home card/link. The launch-type
  option is SDR (a real AWS concept, but the survey records no Fargate
  surface to compare it with).

Digest, Size, Pushed at, Scan status, Vulnerabilities), Permissions
(repository policy JSON editor), Lifecycle rules (rules table: rule
priority, status, action expire), Tags. Create-repository wizard, 5 steps:
(1) name + visibility, (2) tag immutability, (3) scan on push,

(`aws ecr get-login-password | docker login`, `docker tag`, `docker push`).

| Left nav: Images (per repo) | Images card under the selected repository (lines 157–231) | ✅ match |
| Left nav: Lifecycle rules | No nav/tab/table; lifecycle is two fields on the create form (Keep last N tagged, Expire untagged after N days) + an "Evaluate lifecycle rules" button per repository | ⚠️ partial |
| Left nav: Replication | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Left nav: Settings | Absent | ❌ missing |
| List columns: Repository name / URI / Created / Image count | All four present, in order Repository name, URI, Images, Created | ✅ match (4) |

| Detail tab: Permissions | Absent — no repository-policy editor | ❌ missing |
| Detail tab: Lifecycle rules | Same partial as nav (fields + evaluate button, no rules table) | ⚠️ partial |
| Detail tab: Tags | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Images table: Tag / Digest / Size / Scan status | All present (Digest truncated to 19 chars) | ✅ match (4) |
| Images table: Pushed at | Shown only as a line in the image-detail block ("pushed …"), not a column | ⚠️ partial |
| Images table: Vulnerabilities | Absent; scan is an integrity scan (digest re-verification), explicitly "NOT a vulnerability (CVE) scan" | ❌ missing |
| Create wizard (5 steps) | Single inline form: Repository name + Keep last N tagged + Expire untagged after (days) + "Create repository". No visibility, tag immutability, scan-on-push, encryption, or review step | ❌ missing (all 5 steps) |

Repositories table columns, in order: Repository name, URI, Images,
Created, Actions (View images / Delete; **Delete disabled when
image_count > 0**, title "Delete the images first — a non-empty repository
cannot be deleted."). Empty: "No repositories yet. Create one above."
Images table columns, in order: Tag, Digest, Size (bytes), Scan, Actions
(Detail / Scan / Delete). Push-image form: Tag (default "latest"), Layer
content (stored as a blob) + "Push image" + "Evaluate lifecycle rules".
Image detail block (not a tab): tag + digest, size, pushed-at, scan status
+ scan note, "Layers: digests are verified against the stored blobs on
every scan. Layer contents are not shown here (they are binary blobs on
disk)." No search/filter/checkbox/pagination/Refresh/Preferences anywhere.
No error state beyond `ErrorBox` on load failure and the status notice line.

The v3.2 audit does not cover ECR in §3a (ECR is not a lite-61 service).

Preferences gear. No detail tabs, no wizard recorded.

### List page — Stacks table (lines 163–192)
Create form (inline, **not a wizard**): Stack name + Template (JSON or YAML)
textarea (default template: SQS queue + SNS topic) + "Create stack" +
"Validate template" buttons. Validate renders a result line: "Template is
valid / is NOT valid — N resource(s)" + problems + supported-types list. No
template source choice (S3 URL / upload / sample / Designer), no parameters,
no stack options (tags, IAM role, stack policy, rollback config,
notifications), no capabilities acknowledgement, no review step. (Backup,
not survey, records a 4-step wizard: prepare template, stack details,
configure options, review — CloudForge covers name + template only.)

are not shown greyed for 90 days (no deleted-stack listing at all).

### Detail — selected stack (lines 196–232)
No tabs. Three stacked sections in one card "Stack detail — {name}
({status})". (1) Resources table columns: Logical ID, Type, Physical ID —
**no Status column** (backup records Logical ID, Physical ID, Type, Status).
(2) Events table columns: Time, Status, Resource, Reason. (3) "Create change
set": Change set name + updated-template textarea + "Create change set"
button; success notice reports "Change set created: N change(s), M
unsupported, executable: true/false." There is **no Execute button in the
console** — the form note says "Execute a change set from the stack detail
API — the console shows the diff; execution is via the change-set execute
endpoint." No change-set list is shown. Six resource types provision for
real (SQS queue, SNS topic, DynamoDB table, Lambda function, S3 bucket, RDS
instance); any other type is reported UNSUPPORTED in the change set, never
silently skipped. Failed creates roll back the resources they made.
- Detail tabs: — SDR per survey (survey records no detail tabs); backup
  records Overview, Outputs, Resources, Events, Parameters, Template,
  Change sets, Stack policy, Drift detection — of these CloudForge has only
  Resources and Events (as sections, not tabs) and change-set creation; no
  Outputs, Parameters, Template view, Stack policy, or Drift detection.

## 12. EKS — `frontend/src/pages/EksConsole.tsx`, route `/services/eks`

Registry fidelity UNSUPPORTED (`services.go:498`), availability
"unavailable". Console badge UNSUPPORTED, footnote "no cluster exists here".
The console is an **honest empty state by explicit Phase 4 decision** (file
header comment, lines 10–19): no Docker/Kubernetes in the sandbox to verify
against; a managed control plane would dominate lite mode; Kubernetes
concepts map onto the ECS runtime instead. A k3s-based EKS remains a possible
future phase on a real host, gated behind a capability check.

### Survey (recorded)
Home is a marketing page. Left nav: Dashboard, Clusters; Settings (Dashboard
settings, Console settings); EKS Anywhere; Related services. Clusters list
table: Cluster name, Status, Kubernetes version, Support period, Upgrade
policy, Created, Certificate authority, Provider, Actions. Controls: Refresh,
Actions, Delete (disabled), Create cluster, Filter clusters, pagination,
Preferences gear. No detail tabs recorded.

| Settings → Dashboard settings | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Settings → Console settings | Absent | ❌ missing |
| EKS Anywhere | Absent | ❌ missing |
| Related services | Absent | ❌ missing |

"Decision: UNSUPPORTED. {reason}" + "No cluster-info endpoint is offered
because there is no cluster. Creating a cluster is disabled — the button
below is deliberately not wired to anything." + a **disabled** "Create
cluster" button (title "EKS is UNSUPPORTED — there is no cluster to
create.").
- Home page: survey says marketing page; CloudForge shows a Decision card

Empty by design (rows `[]`, totalCount 0). Columns, in order — **all 9

(each rendering "—"). Text above the table: "No clusters — EKS is
UNSUPPORTED in CloudForge and no cluster exists. The table below uses the

Counting rule: each survey-recorded element (nav item, list column, toolbar
control, home-page pattern) is one item; a CloudForge element with no
survey counterpart is "invented"; anything the survey does not record is
SDR and is never counted as a match. ECR and CodeDeploy have no survey
record at all, so their counts are backup-based and shown in brackets —
they must not be compared with the survey-based rows. Fargate has no survey
console at all.

"missing" 7 are the reconciled counts from their full tables (nav + columns + toolbar items).

Survey-based totals (10 services with a survey record, excluding ECR /
CodeDeploy / Fargate): **match 34, partial 24, missing 74, invented 8,
SDR 24**. No Group B service fully matches AWS. The best column-level
matches are EKS Clusters (9/9), CodeBuild projects (4/6 data columns exact,
2 honest "Not available"/local-path partials), CodePipeline pipelines (3/5
exact, 2 partials), CloudFormation stacks (3/4 exact, 1 partial). Every
service is missing most of its nav and its entire toolbar.

## 14. Top gaps (Group B)

1. **No left navigation in any Group B console.** The AWS consoles have
   5–14 nav items each; CloudForge has at most 2 tabs (home/list) or none.
   Worst: CloudFormation 13/14 nav items missing; EventBridge 7 nav items
   missing (Schedules, Schema registry, Pipes, Event streams, Settings,
   Quick starts, CloudWatch link); ECS 7 missing; CodeBuild 6 missing
   (Getting started, Report groups, Report history, Compute fleets,
   Account metrics + Developer Tools grouping).
2. **No toolbar anywhere.** Search/filter, Refresh, Actions dropdown,
   Preferences gear, pagination and checkbox selection are missing in all
   12 services (the `AwsTable` component supports filter + checkboxes, but
   no Group B console wires them). AWS toolbars that are entirely absent
   include CodeBuild's Create trigger / View details / Debug build and
   CodePipeline's Release change / View history.
3. **No wizard anywhere.** All creates are single inline forms. Against the
   backup-recorded AWS wizards: CodeBuild 7 steps → name/source/buildspec
   only; CodePipeline 5 steps → name/source/build/deploy-target only;
   CloudFormation 4 steps → name/template only; ECR 5 steps → name + 2
   lifecycle fields; CodeDeploy create-deployment-group wizard → nothing
   (no groups at all); EKS 5-step create-cluster wizard → a deliberately
   disabled button (honest, by decision).
4. **No detail tabs anywhere.** All detail is stacked cards under a selected
   row (or nothing: ECS and EventBridge rows open no detail at all). The
   survey records no detail tabs, so this is unvalidated rather than proven
   wrong — but the backup-recorded tab sets (ECR Permissions/Tags,
   CodeBuild project detail, CodePipeline Visual/History/Settings,
   CloudFormation Outputs/Parameters/Template/Drift, EKS 7 cluster tabs)
   are all absent.
5. **CodeDeploy has no deployment groups at all** — no group concept, no
   group wizard, no Deployment configurations, no lifecycle-event timeline,
   no target-instances table, no Stop/Retry deployment. The backup note's
   claim that the console shows deployment groups is false (see §10).
6. **EventBridge is rules-only.** Only the default bus exists; no Schedules,
   Schema registry, Pipes, Event streams, Settings, Quick starts, or bus
   management. The "Create event bus" card from the survey has no
   counterpart — the home action creates a rule instead.
7. **API Gateway is REST-only and has no API management surface.** No
   Custom domain names, VPC links, Usage plans, API keys, or API Portal;
   no HTTP API type despite the "REST/HTTP" description; no REST/HTTP/
   WebSocket tab split.
8. **SNS has no Mobile surface and no FIFO topics** (Push/SMS missing;
   email/SMS explicitly not built); **SQS has no FIFO queues** (disabled
   home card). Both are honestly labelled, but they are exam-relevant gaps.
9. **EKS is honestly UNSUPPORTED** (9/9 cluster columns, disabled Create
   cluster, learning-path substitute) — the correct outcome for the Phase 4
   decision, but it means the whole service is a decision page, not a
   console: no Dashboard, Settings, EKS Anywhere, or cluster detail.
10. **Fargate has no console of its own.** `/services/fargate` renders the
    identical ECS console; Fargate exists only as a launch-type option and
    a home-card link. That is arguably the right shape (AWS has no Fargate
    console either), but the route itself is CloudForge-only and nothing
    distinguishes a Fargate run from an ECS run in the UI beyond the
    launch-type label.

## 15. V3.2-audit corrections found while re-checking (Group B)

- **SQS / SNS / EventBridge / API Gateway / ECS (§§1–5):** the v3.2 audit
  lists home-dashboard cards and detail cards as if they were sections/tabs
  ("Messages, Dead-letter queues, FIFO queues", "Subscriptions, Deliveries",
  "Event buses, Archives", "Resources, Stages, WebSocket APIs",
  "Services, Tasks, Fargate (section)"). In the code these are cards on the
  Home dashboard or detail cards under a selected row; each console has at
  most 2 tabs (home/list). The audit's verdicts (mostly/partial matching)
  still stand, but the tab counts are overstated.
- **CodeDeploy backup note (§10):** "Deployment groups" listed as shown in
  the console — false; no deployment-group code exists. "Console already
  labels EC2 compute platform as unavailable" — false; EC2 is never
  mentioned, the target-type select has only ECS/Lambda.
- **ECR backup note (§7):** "lifecycle rules" listed as shown — overstated;
  there is no lifecycle-rules table/tab, only create-form fields plus an
  evaluate button.
- No other v3.2-audit Group B claim was found to be wrong: the survey-column
  assertions for CodeBuild, CodePipeline, CloudFormation and EKS still hold
  exactly as recorded (verified cell by cell above), and the missing-item
  lists for EventBridge, API Gateway, ECS and EKS are confirmed in full.

## 16. What was NOT verified

- No builds or tests were run (per task: no code changes, no builds).
- No live browser check: all findings are from reading the console source
  and the survey/backup documents. Click behaviour (e.g. whether a disabled
  button's title tooltip renders, whether the ECS 5 s task refetch works)
  is as written in the code, not click-tested.
- Backend behaviour was not audited: API routes, validation, and the
  "real vs simulated" substance behind each table are taken from the
  consoles' own comments, banners and fidelity footnotes, not re-verified
  against `backend/`.
- AWS details beyond the survey/backup documents were not invented: where
  the survey is silent (all list columns for SQS/SNS/EventBridge/API
  Gateway/ECS, all detail tabs, all wizard steps), the verdict is SDR.


## Part C — Group C — security/monitoring/management (KMS, Secrets Manager, WAF, GuardDuty, Security Hub, Inspector, Config, Organizations, Cost Explorer, Budgets, Trusted Advisor, SSM, CloudTrail, CloudWatch)

Counts: 15 match, 19 partial, 71 missing, 15 invented, 142 survey-does-not-record

# CloudForge v4 audit — Group C: security, monitoring, management/governance

Date: 2026-11-22. Auditor: subagent (Group C). Scope: KMS, Secrets Manager, WAF,
GuardDuty, Security Hub, Inspector, Config, Organizations, Cost Explorer, Budgets,
Trusted Advisor, SSM / Systems Manager, CloudTrail, CloudWatch.

Sources read first: `docs/reference/aws-console-survey-2026-11-22.md` (main survey,
46/46 services, ap-southeast-2, read-only, no wizard opened),
`docs/reference/aws-policy-editors-survey-2026-11-22.md`,
`docs/reference/tab-audit-full-2026-11-22.md` (v3.2 audit — every claim re-checked
against current code below; several are corrected in §15),
`docs/reference/aws-console-backup/ssm.md` and `.../cloudwatch.md` (public-docs-derived
reference notes, NOT survey observations — verdicts against them are marked PUB).

Code inspected (all current, no builds run, no code changed):
- `frontend/src/pages/SecurityConsoles.tsx` (939 lines: GuardDuty, Security Hub,
  Inspector, Config, WAF, KMS, Secrets Manager)
- `frontend/src/pages/ManagementConsoles.tsx` (557 lines: SSM, Trusted Advisor,
  Budgets, Cost Explorer, Organizations)
- `frontend/src/pages/CloudTrailConsole.tsx` (257 lines)
- `frontend/src/pages/CloudWatchConsole.tsx` (176 lines)
- `frontend/src/components/ServiceHome.tsx` (`ConsoleWithHome`, `ServiceHomeDashboard`)
- `frontend/src/App.tsx` routes; `backend/internal/httpapi/services.go` registry;
  `frontend/src/pages/survey-reconciliation.test.tsx` (what is test-asserted).

## Verdict key

- ✅ **match** — item is in the AWS survey AND in CloudForge with working content.
- ⚠️ **partial** — in both, but CloudForge content is incomplete, disabled, label-only,
  or reduced in scope.
- ❌ **missing** — in the AWS survey, absent in CloudForge.
- ➕ **invented** — in CloudForge, no counterpart in the AWS survey (CloudForge-only UI).
- — **SDNR** (survey does not record) — the survey never captured this, so no verdict
  is possible. Do not read SDNR as a match.
- **PUB** — compared against the backup reference notes, which are derived from public
  console documentation, not from the observed survey. Weaker than a survey verdict.

## Counting method

One row per audited item: each AWS left-nav item, each list-table column, each
toolbar control the survey records, each detail tab, each wizard (as one row —
the survey opened no wizard, so all wizard steps are SDNR by construction), each
Home-dashboard card that is not a duplicate of an already-counted nav item.
A Home card that merely repeats a counted nav item is not double-counted.
Counts in the summary table are the sums of the per-service tables.

## Structural facts that apply to 12 of the 14 services

- `ConsoleWithHome` (`frontend/src/components/ServiceHome.tsx:217-271`) renders a
  **two-item left nav only**: "`<Service> dashboard`" (Home) and one list section
  (e.g. "Customer managed keys"). There is no grouped multi-item AWS-style left nav
  in any Group C console. App routes pass `initialSection="home"`, so users land on
  the CloudForge dashboard first.
- The Home dashboard is the same `ServiceHomeDashboard` pattern everywhere: banner,
  action links, "Resources by Region" cards, Refresh, Service Health lines,
  Settings links, Additional Information links. AWS lands most of these services
  on a marketing home or directly on the list — the CloudForge dashboard itself is
  therefore ➕ invented for every service except where AWS has a real Dashboard/Home
  nav item (CloudTrail, Cost Explorer: ⚠️ partial).
- **SSM and CloudWatch do not use `ConsoleWithHome` at all**: no left nav, no Home
  dashboard — a plain scrolling page under a `PageHeader`
  (`ManagementConsoles.tsx:34`, `CloudWatchConsole.tsx:56`).
- No Group C console has row **detail tabs** (Details / Permissions / Tags / …).
  Row selection opens at most an inline card/pane below the table (KMS encrypt,
  WAF Evaluate, Inspector findings, Organizations members, Secrets value,
  CloudTrail event detail). The survey records detail tabs for **no** Group C
  service either (tables were empty or the list was never reached with rows), so
  all detail-tab verdicts are SDNR — CloudForge's inline-card pattern is its own
  design, unvalidated either way.
- No Group C console has a **search box, checkbox column, pagination, or Preferences
  gear** on any list table. `AwsTable` renders only a "`N of M shown`" footer
  (`frontend/src/components/AwsTable.tsx:62`). The survey records search/pagination/
  Preferences for S3, IAM, Lambda, Budgets, CloudFront etc.; for Group C it records
  them only for Budgets ("Find a budget", pagination, Preferences gear) — all ❌
  missing there.
- No Group C console has a multi-step **create/edit wizard**. All creation is a
  single inline form above the table (name + 1–3 fields). The survey opened no
  wizard for any service, so every wizard verdict is SDNR — but the *absence* of
  any wizard is itself a structural gap vs AWS, where wizards are the normal
  create path (KMS Create key, Secrets "Store a new secret", WAF Get started,
  Budgets Create budget).

## Cross-cutting defect found while auditing (all Group C, not in v3.2 audit)

**Literal JavaScript source rendered as a Health line on 7 Home dashboards.**
In `SecurityConsoles.tsx:296` (Inspector), `:431` (Config), `:583` (WAF),
`:737` (KMS), `:873` (Secrets) and `ManagementConsoles.tsx:166` (Trusted Advisor),
`:479` (Organizations), the first `health` entry is a **double-quoted string
containing code**, e.g. `"assessments.isError ? 'Assessments could not be loaded.' :
\`${assessRows.length} assessment run(s) listed.\`"` — not a template literal.
`ServiceHomeDashboard` renders `health` strings verbatim, so the Home dashboard
shows the literal text `assessments.isError ? 'Assessments could not be loaded.' :
${assessRows.length} assessment run(s) listed.` instead of a count or an error.
GuardDuty (`SecurityConsoles.tsx:115`), Security Hub (`:206`), Cost Explorer
(`ManagementConsoles.tsx:346`) and CloudTrail use real backtick template literals
and are unaffected. This is visible on every load of those 7 dashboards, error or
not — it is not a conditional rendering bug.

"SIMULATED (console) / PARTIAL (budgets)" note.

(The page auto-refreshes monitoring every 15 s; there is no manual Refresh.)

### 14b. What the page does have

Three stacked cards, in order:

1. **Component health** (`:86-100`): columns Component, Status (badge),
   Latency, Detail. ➕ invented — CloudForge-only (live `/readyz` checks);
   no AWS counterpart in the survey. Footer line: API uptime, version, mode
   (lite/full), note.
2. **Alarms** (`:102-152`): inline create form — Alarm name, Metric dropdown
   (populated from observed metric names only), Comparison (>/</), Threshold —
   + "Create alarm" (disabled while name empty or no metric). Table columns:
   Alarm, Metric, Rule (`> 0.5`), Current value, State (badge), Actions
   (Delete link). Empty state "No alarms yet — create one above." Alarms are
   in-memory, lost on API restart (labelled, `:128-131`); creating one on a
   never-observed metric is rejected. **Survey records no CloudWatch table
   columns** → all 6 alarm columns SDNR. Vs the backup notes (PUB): AWS alarm
   list is Alarm name, State, Threshold, Actions, Last state change — CloudForge
   has name/state/threshold-as-Rule, no Actions config (SNS/EC2/Auto Scaling),
   no Last state change; no alarm Detail/History tabs. The 5-step Create-alarm
   wizard in the backup (select metric → conditions → actions → name → preview)
   is an inline 4-field form here — ⚠️ partial (PUB): fields exist, no steps,
   no statistic/period/datapoints/missing-data treatment, no actions.
3. **Metrics** (`:153-175`): filter input (substring on metric name) + "Refreshed
   every 15s" note. Table columns: Metric, Type, Total, Samples, Help.
   **Survey records no CloudWatch metric columns** → all 5 SDNR. No namespace
   tree, no graph composer, no statistic/period picker, no "Add to dashboard",
   no "Create alarm from metric" (alarm creation is a separate form). No Logs
   of any kind anywhere in the file (grep for "log" finds nothing except
   component names) — despite the registry description "Metrics, alarms and
   logs over the local control plane" (`services.go:711`).

### 14c. Backup-file claim is FALSE for CloudWatch

`docs/reference/aws-console-backup/cloudwatch.md` §3 ("CloudForge fidelity today")
claims the current console shows "Metrics / Alarms / **Logs / Dashboards left
nav; metric graphs; Create-alarm wizard**". Against the actual file: there is no
left nav at all, no Logs, no Dashboards, no metric graphs, and no wizard — only
the three stacked cards above. That backup claim must not be relied on; it
appears to describe an intended console, not the built one. (The backup file's
§4 build plan — persist alarms, add metric history, add logs — is still valid
and still unbuilt.)

|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 2 | 18 | 1 | 14 |

Partial = Alarms, Metrics nav. Missing = the other 18 nav items. Invented =

form + detail tabs + empty state.

## 15. Re-checks of v3.2-audit claims (corrections)

The v3.2 audit's §3a table is broadly accurate for Group C, but these claims do
not survive a re-read of the current code:

1. **Budgets routing — v3.2 fix CONFIRMED.** "No dedicated route — falls back to
   generic ServiceDetail" is now false: `/services/budgets` renders the real
   Budgets table (§10a). The remaining gaps are the missing Reports tab, search,
   pagination, Preferences, and checkbox — not routing.
2. **"Home + Detectors, Findings, Suppression rules" (GuardDuty)** — overstates:
   Suppression rules is a disabled Home card only (`SecurityConsoles.tsx:121`);
   there is no section, list, or rule editor. Same pattern for Secrets Manager
   ("Versions, Rotation schedules"), KMS ("Aliases, Grants"), WAF ("Rules, Rule
   groups, Shield"), Security Hub ("Open findings, Standards"), Inspector
   ("Findings, Assessment templates"), Organizations ("Accounts, Service control
   policies"), Trusted Advisor ("Check categories"), Cost Explorer ("Savings
   Plans"): in every case the second and third items are **Home cards only**, not
   sections. Only Config's three items (Rules, Evaluation results, Configuration
   items) are all real tables — and even those are three cards stacked on one
   list page, not nav sections. Any v4 counting must count cards as cards, not
   as consoles' sections.
3. **KMS "Aliases" card note is false** ("Aliases on the key detail",
   `SecurityConsoles.tsx:727-728`): no alias UI or backend exists (§1b). The
   v3.2 audit did not catch this.
4. **CloudWatch backup claim** (§14c): the backup file, not the v3.2 audit, claims
   a Logs/Dashboards nav, graphs, and a wizard that do not exist. The v3.2 audit
   itself correctly records CloudWatch only as "Overview dashboard … No table on
   Overview" from the survey; it never verified the console against that.
5. **Fidelity**: the v3.2 audit's "SIMULATED (console) / PARTIAL (budgets)" for
   Cost Explorer is still the true state; the registry still says PARTIAL for
   Cost Explorer, so the registry/console mismatch persists (§9, fidelity table).
6. **"Mostly matching" labels in the v3.2 audit for KMS / Secrets Manager /
   CloudTrail** rest on nav-label presence, not on content: KMS matches 1 of 5
   nav items, Secrets Manager's single nav item matches but its detail/versions/
   rotation surfaces are cards only, CloudTrail matches 1 of 12 nav items with
   0/3 event columns. The labels are not wrong as shorthand, but v4 should not
   repeat them without the numbers (§16).

(19), CloudTrail (16) and WAF (9) — the four services whose AWS nav the survey
actually recorded in full. "Match 15" is dominated by Budgets (7: tab + 6 columns)
and Cost Explorer's breakdown table (3). The 153 SDNR items are the real story of
